each perturbation type (or nominal), and saves per-layer\\\\nactivations with 4 pooling strategies in fp16.\\\\n\\\\nUsage:\\\\n    # Collect camera perturbation episodes\\\\n    python   --num_episodes 50 \\\\\\\\\\\\n        --device cuda:0\\\\n\\\\n    # Collect all perturbation types\\\\n    python collect_activations.py \\\\\\\\\\\\n        --checkpoint /path/to/pi0_base \\\\\\\\\\\\n        --output_dir ./activations \\\\\\\\\\\\n        --perturbation_type all \\\\\\\\\\\\n        --num_episodes 50\\\\n\\\\n    # Collect nominal (no perturbation) baseline\\\\n    python collect_activations.py \\\\\\\\\\\\n        --checkpoint /path/to/pi0_base \\\\\\\\\\\\n        --output_dir ./activations \\\\\\\\\\\\n        --perturbation_type nominal \\\\\\\\\\\\n        --num_episodes 100\\\\n\\\\nDependencies:\\\\n    torch, safetensors, openpi (for model loading), libero\\\\n\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\nimport argparse\\\\nimport logging\\\\nimport os\\\\nfrom pathlib import Path\\\\nfrom typing import Any\\\\n\\\\nimport torch\\\\nimport torch.nn as nn\\\\n\\\\nfrom configs import (\\\\n    CLASS_6_TO_3_MAPPING,\\\\n    EXPERT_LAYER_TEMPLATE,\\\\n    EXPERT_LAYERS,\\\\n    PALIGEMMA_LAYER_TEMPLATE,\\\\n    PALIGEMMA_LAYERS,\\\\n    PERTURBATION_TYPES_6CLASS,\\\\n    POOLING_STRATEGIES,\\\\n)\\\\nfrom libero_integration import (\\\\n    load_pi0_for_collection,\\\\n    obs_to_model_input,\\\\n    run_episode,\\\\n)\\\\n\\\\nlog = logging.getLogger(__name__)\\\\n\\\\n\\\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\\\n# Pooling helpers\\\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\\\n\\\\ndef pool_activation(hidden_states: torch.Tensor, strategy: str) -> torch.Tensor:\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Pool a (batch, seq_len, hidden_dim) tensor into (batch, D).\\\\n\\\\n    Strategies:\\\\n        mean       — mean over seq_len → (batch, hidden_dim)\\\\n        first      — first token       → (batch, hidden_dim)\\\\n        last       — last token        → (batch, hidden_dim)\\\\n        first_last — concat first+last → (batch, hidden_dim*2)\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    if strategy == \\\\\\\"mean\\\\\\\":\\\\n        return hidden_states.mean(dim=1)\\\\n    elif strategy == \\\\\\\"first\\\\\\\":\\\\n        return hidden_states[:, 0]\\\\n    elif strategy == \\\\\\\"last\\\\\\\":\\\\n        return hidden_states[:, -1]\\\\n    elif strategy == \\\\\\\"first_last\\\\\\\":\\\\n        return torch.cat([hidden_states[:, 0], hidden_states[:, -1]], dim=-1)\\\\n    raise ValueError(f\\\\\\\"Unknown pooling strategy: {strategy}\\\\\\\")\\\\n\\\\n\\\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\\\n# Hook manager\\\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\\\n\\\\nclass ActivationCollector:\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Registers hooks on specified layers and stores pooled activations.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n    def __init__(self, model: nn.Module):\\\\n        self.model = model\\\\n        self._hooks: list[torch.utils.hooks.RemovableHook] = []\\\\n        self._raw: dict[str, torch.Tensor] = {}  # layer_name -> last hidden_states\\\\n\\\\n    def register_hooks(self) -> None:\\\\n        for i in PALIGEMMA_LAYERS:\\\\n            name = f\\\\\\\"paligemma_layer_{i}\\\\\\\"\\\\n            module_path = PALIGEMMA_LAYER_TEMPLATE.format(i=i)\\\\n            module = _get_submodule(self.model, module_path)\\\\n            self._hooks.append(\\\\n                module.register_forward_hook(self._make_hook(name))\\\\n            )\\\\n            log.info(\\\\\\\"Hooked %s -> %s\\\\\\\", name, module_path)\\\\n\\\\n        for i in EXPERT_LAYERS:\\\\n            name = f\\\\\\\"expert_layer_{i}\\\\\\\"\\\\n            module_path = EXPERT_LAYER_TEMPLATE.format(i=i)\\\\n            module = _get_submodule(self.model, module_path)\\\\n            self._hooks.append(\\\\n                module.register_forward_hook(self._make_hook(name))\\\\n            )\\\\n            log.info(\\\\\\\"Hooked %s -> %s\\\\\\\", name, module_path)\\\\n\\\\n    def _make_hook(self, name: str):\\\\n        def hook_fn(_module, _input, output):\\\\n            # Gemma layers return (hidden_states, ...) or just hidden_states\\\\n            if isinstance(output, tuple):\\\\n                h = output[0]\\\\n         \\\\n\\\\n... random.seed(seed)\\\\n    np.random.seed(seed)\\\\n    torch.manual_seed(seed)\\\\n    if torch.cuda.is_available():\\\\n        torch.cuda.manual_seed_all(seed)\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef collect(args: # camera + light + background + noise\\n    \\\"spatial\\\": 1,  # layout\\n    \\\"physical\\\": 2, # robot\\n}\\n\\nCLASS_6_TO_3_MAPPING = {\\n    0: 0,  # camera_viewpoints   -> visual\\n    1: 0, \\\"objects_layout\\\",\\n    \\\"robot_initial_states\\\",\\n]\\n\\nCLASS_3_NAMES = [\\\"visual\\\", \\\"spatial\\\", \\\"physical\\\"]\\n\\nBINARY_NAMES = [\\\"nominal\\\", \\\"perturbed\\\"]\\n\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n# pi0 architecture constants\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n\\nPALIGEMMA_LAYERS = [2, 4, 6, 8, 10, 12, 14, 16]\\nEXPERT_LAYERS = [2, 6, 10, 14]\\n\\nPALIGEMMA_HIDDEN_DIM = 2048\\nEXPERT_HIDDEN_DIM = 1024\\n\\nPALIGEMMA_NUM_LAYERS = 18  # layers[0..17]\\nEXPERT_NUM_LAYERS = 18\\n\\n# PyTorch module path templates\\nPALIGEMMA_LAYER_TEMPLATE = (\\n    \\\"paligemma_with_expert.paligemma.language_model.layers.{i}\\\"\\n)\\nEXPERT_LAYER_TEMPLATE = (\\n    \\\"paligemma_with_expert.gemma_expert.model.layers.{i}\\\"\\n)\\n\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n\\nPOOLING_STRATEGIES = [\\\"mean\\\", \\\"first\\\", \\\"last\\\", \\\"first_last\\\"]\\n\\ndef ex_dim = len(EXPERT_LAYERS) * pooling_output_dim(pooling, EXPERT_HIDDEN_DIM)\\n    if activation_source == \\\"paligemma_only\\\":\\n        return pg_dim\\n    elif activation_source == \\\"expert_only\\\":\\n        return ex_dim\\n    elif activation_source == \\\"both\\\":\\n        return pg_dim + ex_dim\\n    raise ValueError(f\\\"Unknown activation_source: {activation_source}\\\")\\n\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n# Sweep grid\\n# \\\"expert_only\\\", \\\"both\\\"]\\nSWEEP_LRS = [1e-2, 1e-3, 1e-4]\\n\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n# Go / No-Go thresholds (Phase 0 success    \\\"3class_macro_f1_augment\\\": 0.50, # macro-F1 50-70% -> AUGMENT (add proprioception + visual change)\\n    \\\"6class_macro_f1_viable\\\": 0.50,  # macro-F1 >50% -> finer-grained routing \\\\\\\"libero_goal\\\\\\\": 300,\\\\n    \\\\\\\"libero_10\\\\\\\": 520,\\\\n    \\\\\\\"libero_90\\\\\\\": 400,\\\\n}\\\\n\\\\nVALID_PERTURBATION_TYPES = [\\\\n    \\\\\\\"camera_viewpoints\\\\\\\",\\\\n    \\\\\\\"light_conditions\\\\\\\",\\\\n    \\\\\\\"background_textures\\\\\\\",\\\\n    \\\\\\\"objects_layout\\\\\\\",\\\\n helpers\\\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\\\n\\\\ndef _quat2axisangle(quat: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Quaternion [x,y,z,w] -> axis-angle [ax,ay,az].\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    q = np.asarray(quat, dtype=np.float64)\\\\n    norm = np.linalg.norm(q)\\\\n    if norm < 1e-8:\\\\n        return np.zeros(3, dtype=np.float32)\\\\n    q /= norm\\\\n    sin_half = np.linalg.norm(q[:3])\\\\n    if sin_half < 1e-8:\\\\n        return np.zeros(3, dtype=np.float32)\\\\n    angle = 2.0 * math.atan2(sin_half, preprocessing\\\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\\\n\\\\ndef _resize_with_pad(img: np.ndarray, size: int) -> np.ndarray:\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Resize image with aspect-preserving padding to (size, size, 3) uint8.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    try:\\\\n        from openpi_client.image_tools import resize_with_pad, convert_to_uint8\\\\n        return convert_to_uint8(resize_with_pad(img, size, size))\\\\n    except ImportError:\\\\n        from PIL import Image as PILImage\\\\n        h, w = img.shape[:2]\\\\n        scale = min(size / h, size / w)\\\\n        new_h, new_w = int(h * scale), int(w * scale)\\\\n        resized = np.array(\\\\n            PILImage.fromarray(img).resize((new_w, new_h), PILImage.BILINEAR)\\\\n        )\\\\n        canvas = np.zeros((size, size, 3), dtype=np.uint8)\\\\n        y0, x0 = (size - new_h) // 2, (size - new_w) // 2\\\\n        canvas[y0 : y0 + new_h, x0 : x0 + new_w] = resized\\\\n        return canvas\\\\n\\\\n\\\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\\\n# Observation -> model input\\\\n# Activations are averaged across all replan points.\\\\n\\\\n    Sensor noise is applied by the LIBERO-Plus environment itself (via task\\\\n    name suffix _noise_N), not at the observation level.\\\\n\\\\n    Returns dict with keys: activations, task_name, task_description,\\\\n    num_steps, success.\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    env, task_description, init_states, task_idx = create_libero_env(\\\\n        task_suite_name=task_suite,\\\\n        perturbation_type=perturbation_type,\\\\n        seed=seed,\\\\n    )\\\\n\\\\n    max_steps = MAX_STEPS.get(task_suite, 400)\\\\n\\\\n    step = 0\\\\n    success = False\\\\n\\\\n    try:\\\\n        trial_idx = random.randint(0, len(init_states) - 1)\\\\n        obs = env.set_init_state(init_states[trial_idx])\\\\n\\\\n        for _ in range(num_steps_wait):\\\\n            obs, _, _, _ = env.step(LIBERO_DUMMY_ACTION)\\\\n\\\\n        action_plan: collections.deque = collections.deque()\\\\n        step_activations: list[dict] = []\\\\n        done = False\\\\n\\\\n        while not done and step < max_steps:\\\\n            if len(action_plan) == 0:\\\\n                collector.clear()\\\\n\\\\n                obs_dict = obs_to_model_input(obs, task_description)\\\\n                result = policy.infer(obs_dict)\\\\n                action_chunk = result[\\\\\\\"actions\\\\\\\"]  # [horizon, 7]\\\\n                action_plan.extend(action_chunk[:replan_steps])\\\\n\\\\n                pooled = collector.get_pooled()\\\\n                if pooled:\\\\n                    step_activations.append(pooled)\\\\n\\\\n            action = action_plan.popleft()\\\\n            if isinstance(action, torch.Tensor):\\\\n                action = action.cpu().numpy()\\\\n            obs, reward, done, info = env.step(\\\\n                action.tolist() if hasattr(action, \\\\\\\"tolist\\\\\\\") else list(action)\\\\n            )\\\\n            step += 1\\\\n\\\\n            if done or reward > 0:\\\\n                success = info.get(\\\\\\\"success\\\\\\\", reward > 0)\\\\n    finally:\\\\n        env.close()\\\\n\\\\n    return {\\\\n        \\\\\\\"activations\\\\\\\": _average_activations(step_activations),\\\\n        \\\\\\\"task_name\\\\\\\": f\\\\\\\"{task_suite}/task_{task_idx}\\\\\\\",\\\\n        \\\\\\\"task_description\\\\\\\": task_description,\\\\n        \\\\\\\"num_steps\\\\\\\": step,\\\\n        \\\\\\\"success\\\\\\\": success,\\\\n    }\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef _average_activations(\\\\n    step_activations: list[dict[str, dict[str, torch.Tensor]]],\\\\n) -> dict[str, dict[str, torch.Tensor]]:\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Average activation tensors across replan steps.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    if not step_activations:\\\\n        return {}\\\\n    result: dict[str, dict[str, torch.Tensor]] = {}\\\\n    for layer in step_activations[0]:\\\\n        result[layer] = {}\\\\n        for strat in step_activations[0][layer]:\\\\n            tensors = [sa[layer][strat] for sa in step_activations]\\\\n            result[layer][strat] = torch.stack(tensors).mean(dim=0)\\\\n    return result\\\\n\\\", \\\"stderr\\\": \\\"\\\", \\\"return_code\\\": 0}, \\\"error\\\": null, \\\"metadata\\\": {\\\"elapsed_seconds\\\": 0.49}}\"}","structuredContent":{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"\\\"\\\"\\\"\\nOpenPI/LIBERO-Plus integration for pi0 activation collection.\\n\\nBridges LIBERO-Plus simulation environments with the pi0 model, handling\\nobservation preprocessing, action chunking, and perturbation-specific\\ntask selection. Designed for direct model inference (no WebSocket server)\\nso that ActivationCollector hooks fire during forward passes.\\n\\nInput:\\n    LIBERO-Plus env observations: agentview_image [256,256,3],\\n    robot0_eye_in_hand_image [256,256,3], robot0_eef_pos [3],\\n    robot0_eef_quat [4], robot0_gripper_qpos [2], plus task language.\\n\\nOutput:\\n    Per-episode activation dict (via ActivationCollector hooks on the\\n    underlying PI0Pytorch model) and task metadata.\\n\\nIntegration with collect_activations.py:\\n    from libero_integration import load_pi0_for_collection, run_episode\\n\\n    policy, model = load_pi0_for_collection(checkpoint_path, device)\\n    collector = ActivationCollector(model)\\n    collector.register_hooks()\\n    result = run_episode(policy, collector, \\\"camera_viewpoints\\\", device)\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\nimport collections\\nimport logging\\nimport math\\nimport re\\nimport random\\nfrom pathlib import Path\\nfrom typing import Any\\n\\nimport numpy as np\\nimport torch\\nimport torch.nn as nn\\n\\nlog = logging.getLogger(__name__)\\n\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n# Constants (from OpenPI's examples/libero/main.py)\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n\\nLIBERO_DUMMY_ACTION = [0.0] * 6 + [-1.0]\\nLIBERO_ENV_RESOLUTION = 256\\nMODEL_INPUT_RESOLUTION = 224\\nDEFAULT_REPLAN_STEPS = 5\\nDEFAULT_NUM_STEPS_WAIT = 10\\n\\nMAX_STEPS = {\\n    \\\"libero_spatial\\\": 220,\\n    \\\"libero_object\\\": 280,\\n    \\\"libero_goal\\\": 300,\\n    \\\"libero_10\\\": 520,\\n    \\\"libero_90\\\": 400,\\n}\\n\\nVALID_PERTURBATION_TYPES = [\\n    \\\"camera_viewpoints\\\",\\n    \\\"light_conditions\\\",\\n    \\\"background_textures\\\",\\n    \\\"objects_layout\\\",\\n    \\\"robot_initial_states\\\",\\n    \\\"sensor_noise\\\",\\n]\\n\\n\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n# Geometry helpers\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n\\ndef _quat2axisangle(quat: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Quaternion [x,y,z,w] -> axis-angle [ax,ay,az].\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    q = np.asarray(quat, dtype=np.float64)\\n    norm = np.linalg.norm(q)\\n    if norm < 1e-8:\\n        return np.zeros(3, dtype=np.float32)\\n    q /= norm\\n    sin_half = np.linalg.norm(q[:3])\\n    if sin_half < 1e-8:\\n        return np.zeros(3, dtype=np.float32)\\n    angle = 2.0 * math.atan2(sin_half, q[3])\\n    axis = q[:3] / sin_half\\n    return (axis * angle).astype(np.float32)\\n\\n\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n# Image preprocessing\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n\\ndef _resize_with_pad(img: np.ndarray, size: int) -> np.ndarray:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Resize image with aspect-preserving padding to (size, size, 3) uint8.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    try:\\n        from openpi_client.image_tools import resize_with_pad, convert_to_uint8\\n        return convert_to_uint8(resize_with_pad(img, size, size))\\n    except ImportError:\\n        from PIL import Image as PILImage\\n        h, w = img.shape[:2]\\n        scale = min(size / h, size / w)\\n        new_h, new_w = int(h * scale), int(w * scale)\\n        resized = np.array(\\n            PILImage.fromarray(img).resize((new_w, new_h), PILImage.BILINEAR)\\n        )\\n        canvas = np.zeros((size, size, 3), dtype=np.uint8)\\n        y0, x0 = (size - new_h) // 2, (size - new_w) // 2\\n        canvas[y0 : y0 + new_h, x0 : x0 + new_w] = resized\\n        return canvas\\n\\n\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n# Observation -> model input\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n\\ndef obs_to_model_input(\\n    obs: dict,\\n    task_description: str,\\n    resize_size: int = MODEL_INPUT_RESOLUTION,\\n) -> dict:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Convert LIBERO observation to the dict format expected by policy.infer().\\n\\n    LIBERO obs keys used:\\n        agentview_image          \\n\\n... [截断: 输出过长，省略 7871 字符] ...\\n\\ne, num_tasks)\\n\\n    if task_idx is not None and task_idx in valid_indices:\\n        selected_idx = task_idx\\n    else:\\n        selected_idx = random.choice(valid_indices)\\n\\n    task = suite.get_task(selected_idx)\\n    task_description = task.language\\n\\n    task_bddl_file = (\\n        Path(get_libero_path(\\\"bddl_files\\\")) / task.problem_folder / task.bddl_file\\n    )\\n    env = OffScreenRenderEnv(\\n        bddl_file_name=str(task_bddl_file),\\n        camera_heights=LIBERO_ENV_RESOLUTION,\\n        camera_widths=LIBERO_ENV_RESOLUTION,\\n    )\\n    env.seed(seed)\\n\\n    init_states = suite.get_task_init_states(selected_idx)\\n    return env, task_description, init_states, selected_idx\\n\\n\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n# Episode rollout\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n\\ndef run_episode(\\n    policy: object,\\n    collector: object,\\n    perturbation_type: str | None,\\n    device: str,\\n    task_suite: str = \\\"libero_spatial\\\",\\n    seed: int = 42,\\n    replan_steps: int = DEFAULT_REPLAN_STEPS,\\n    num_steps_wait: int = DEFAULT_NUM_STEPS_WAIT,\\n) -> dict[str, Any]:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Run one LIBERO-Plus episode, collecting activations at each replan point.\\n\\n    Every replan_steps env steps, the policy is queried for a new action chunk.\\n    ActivationCollector hooks capture layer outputs during each policy.infer()\\n    call. Activations are averaged across all replan points.\\n\\n    Sensor noise is applied by the LIBERO-Plus environment itself (via task\\n    name suffix _noise_N), not at the observation level.\\n\\n    Returns dict with keys: activations, task_name, task_description,\\n    num_steps, success.\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    env, task_description, init_states, task_idx = create_libero_env(\\n        task_suite_name=task_suite,\\n        perturbation_type=perturbation_type,\\n        seed=seed,\\n    )\\n\\n    max_steps = MAX_STEPS.get(task_suite, 400)\\n\\n    step = 0\\n    success = False\\n\\n    try:\\n        trial_idx = random.randint(0, len(init_states) - 1)\\n        obs = env.set_init_state(init_states[trial_idx])\\n\\n        for _ in range(num_steps_wait):\\n            obs, _, _, _ = env.step(LIBERO_DUMMY_ACTION)\\n\\n        action_plan: collections.deque = collections.deque()\\n        step_activations: list[dict] = []\\n        done = False\\n\\n        while not done and step < max_steps:\\n            if len(action_plan) == 0:\\n                collector.clear()\\n\\n                obs_dict = obs_to_model_input(obs, task_description)\\n                result = policy.infer(obs_dict)\\n                action_chunk = result[\\\"actions\\\"]  # [horizon, 7]\\n                action_plan.extend(action_chunk[:replan_steps])\\n\\n                pooled = collector.get_pooled()\\n                if pooled:\\n                    step_activations.append(pooled)\\n\\n            action = action_plan.popleft()\\n            if isinstance(action, torch.Tensor):\\n                action = action.cpu().numpy()\\n            obs, reward, done, info = env.step(\\n                action.tolist() if hasattr(action, \\\"tolist\\\") else list(action)\\n            )\\n            step += 1\\n\\n            if done or reward > 0:\\n                success = info.get(\\\"success\\\", reward > 0)\\n    finally:\\n        env.close()\\n\\n    return {\\n        \\\"activations\\\": _average_activations(step_activations),\\n        \\\"task_name\\\": f\\\"{task_suite}/task_{task_idx}\\\",\\n        \\\"task_description\\\": task_description,\\n        \\\"num_steps\\\": step,\\n        \\\"success\\\": success,\\n    }\\n\\n\\ndef _average_activations(\\n    step_activations: list[dict[str, dict[str, torch.Tensor]]],\\n) -> dict[str, dict[str, torch.Tensor]]:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Average activation tensors across replan steps.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    if not step_activations:\\n        return {}\\n    result: dict[str, dict[str, torch.Tensor]] = {}\\n    for layer in step_activations[0]:\\n        result[layer] = {}\\n        for strat in step_activations[0][layer]:\\n            tensors = [sa[layer][strat] for sa in step_activations]\\n            result[layer][strat] = torch.stack(tensors).mean(dim=0)\\n    return result\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.49}}"}}}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-6","id":"msg_01Rssk5L2vz7iit6HGCwh6LZ","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01WZrdZtwjVLKvYf42jc3xfZ","name":"mcp__agent-ml-research__ssh_execute","input":{"server":"westd-34142","command":"cat > /root/meta_brain_lora_routing/phase0/collect_activations_v2.py << 'SCRIPT'\n\"\"\"\nCollect per-step pi0_libero activations for PAR Router v2 retraining.\n\nSaves per-replan-point activations (not averaged) in the same format\nas activations/per_step/*.pt for direct use with train_par_router.py.\n\nUsage:\n    cd /root/meta_brain_lora_routing/phase0\n    python collect_activations_v2.py \\\n        --checkpoint /root/autodl-tmp/checkpoints/pi0_libero \\\n        --output_dir /root/meta_brain_lora_routing/activations_v2/per_step \\\n        --perturbation_type all \\\n        --num_episodes 25 \\\n        --device cuda:1\n\"\"\"\n\nimport argparse\nimport collections\nimport logging\nimport random\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nimport numpy as np\nimport torch\n\nfrom configs import (\n    CLASS_6_TO_3_MAPPING,\n    PERTURBATION_TYPES_6CLASS,\n)\nfrom libero_integration import (\n    create_libero_env,\n    load_pi0_for_collection,\n    obs_to_model_input,\n    LIBERO_DUMMY_ACTION,\n    MAX_STEPS,\n    DEFAULT_REPLAN_STEPS,\n    DEFAULT_NUM_STEPS_WAIT,\n)\n\nlog = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n\n\ndef run_episode_per_step(\n    policy,\n    collector,\n    perturbation_type: str | None,\n    device: str,\n    task_suite: str = \"libero_spatial\",\n    seed: int = 42,\n    replan_steps: int = DEFAULT_REPLAN_STEPS,\n    num_steps_wait: int = DEFAULT_NUM_STEPS_WAIT,\n) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"Run one episode, returning per-step activations (not averaged).\"\"\"\n    env, task_description, init_states, task_idx = create_libero_env(\n        task_suite_name=task_suite,\n        perturbation_type=perturbation_type,\n        seed=seed,\n    )\n    max_steps = MAX_STEPS.get(task_suite, 400)\n    step = 0\n    success = False\n\n    try:\n        trial_idx = random.randint(0, len(init_states) - 1)\n        obs = env.set_init_state(init_states[trial_idx])\n\n        for _ in range(num_steps_wait):\n            obs, _, _, _ = env.step(LIBERO_DUMMY_ACTION)\n\n        action_plan = collections.deque()\n        step_activations = []\n        done = False\n\n        while not done and step < max_steps:\n            if len(action_plan) == 0:\n                collector.clear()\n                obs_dict = obs_to_model_input(obs, task_description)\n                result = policy.infer(obs_dict)\n                action_chunk = result[\"actions\"]\n                action_plan.extend(action_chunk[:replan_steps])\n\n                pooled = collector.get_pooled()\n                if pooled:\n                    step_activations.append(pooled)\n\n            action = action_plan.popleft()\n            if isinstance(action, torch.Tensor):\n                action = action.cpu().numpy()\n            obs, reward, done, info = env.step(\n                action.tolist() if hasattr(action, \"tolist\") else list(action)\n            )\n            step += 1\n            if done or reward > 0:\n                success = info.get(\"success\", reward > 0)\n    finally:\n        env.close()\n\n    return {\n        \"step_activations\": step_activations,\n        \"num_replan_points\": len(step_activations),\n        \"task_name\": f\"{task_suite}/task_{task_idx}\",\n        \"task_description\": task_description,\n        \"num_steps\": step,\n        \"success\": success,\n    }\n\n\ndef make_labels(perturbation_type: str) -> dict:\n    if perturbation_type == \"nominal\":\n        return {\"label_6class\": -1, \"label_3class\": -1, \"label_binary\": 0}\n    label_6 = PERTURBATION_TYPES_6CLASS[perturbation_type]\n    label_3 = CLASS_6_TO_3_MAPPING[label_6]\n    return {\"label_6class\": label_6, \"label_3class\": label_3, \"label_binary\": 1}\n\n\ndef collect(args):\n    random.seed(args.seed)\n    np.random.seed(args.seed)\n    torch.manual_seed(args.seed)\n\n    device = args.device\n    output_dir = Path(args.output_dir)\n    output_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n\n    log.info(\"Loading pi0_libero from %s on %s\", args.checkpoint, device)\n    policy, model = load_pi0_for_collection(args.checkpoint, device)\n\n    from collect_activations import ActivationCollector\n    collector = ActivationCollector(model)\n    collector.register_hooks()\n\n    if args.perturbation_type == \"all\":\n        perturbation_types = list(PERTURBATION_TYPES_6CLASS.keys())\n    else:\n        perturbation_types = [args.perturbation_type]\n\n    for ptype in perturbation_types:\n        is_nominal = ptype == \"nominal\"\n        log.info(\"Collecting %d episodes for %s\", args.num_episodes, ptype)\n        episodes = []\n\n        for ep_idx in range(args.num_episodes):\n            log.info(\"  Episode %d/%d\", ep_idx + 1, args.num_episodes)\n            try:\n                result = run_episode_per_step(\n                    policy, collector,\n                    perturbation_type=None if is_nominal else ptype,\n                    device=device,\n                    task_suite=args.task_suite,\n                    seed=args.seed + ep_idx,\n                )\n            except Exception:\n                log.exception(\"Episode %d failed, skipping\", ep_idx)\n                continue\n\n            labels = make_labels(ptype)\n            episode_data = {\n                \"step_activations\": result[\"step_activations\"],\n                \"num_replan_points\": result[\"num_replan_points\"],\n                **labels,\n                \"metadata\": {\n                    \"perturbation_type\": ptype,\n                    \"task_name\": result[\"task_name\"],\n                    \"num_steps\": result[\"num_steps\"],\n                    \"success\": result[\"success\"],\n                    \"episode_idx\": ep_idx,\n                },\n            }\n            episodes.append(episode_data)\n\n        save_path = output_dir / f\"{ptype}.pt\"\n        torch.save(episodes, save_path)\n        total_steps = sum(ep[\"num_replan_points\"] for ep in episodes)\n        log.info(\"Saved %s: %d episodes, %d total steps\", save_path, len(episodes), total_steps)\n\n    collector.remove_hooks()\n    log.info(\"Collection complete.\")\n\n\ndef parse_args():\n    p = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=\"Collect pi0_libero per-step activations\")\n    p.add_argument(\"--checkpoint\", type=str,\n                   default=\"/root/autodl-tmp/checkpoints/pi0_libero\")\n    p.add_argument(\"--output_dir\", type=str,\n                   default=\"/root/meta_brain_lora_routing/activations_v2/per_step\")\n    valid_types = list(PERTURBATION_TYPES_6CLASS.keys()) + [\"all\", \"nominal\"]\n    p.add_argument(\"--perturbation_type\", type=str, default=\"all\", choices=valid_types)\n    p.add_argument(\"--num_episodes\", type=int, default=25)\n    p.add_argument(\"--task_suite\", type=str, default=\"libero_spatial\",\n                   choices=[\"libero_spatial\", \"libero_object\", \"libero_goal\", \"libero_10\", \"libero_90\"])\n    p.add_argument(\"--device\", type=str, default=\"cuda:1\")\n    p.add_argument(\"--seed\", type=int, default=42)\n    return p.parse_args()\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    logging.basicConfig(\n        level=logging.INFO,\n        format=\"%(asctime)s [%(levelname)s] %(name)s: %(message)s\",\n    )\n    args = parse_args()\n    collect(args)\nSCRIPT","account":"root"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":665,"cache_read_input_tokens":46786,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":665},"output_tokens":8,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"436bc48d-6197-44c5-bb0b-d9d9fb2582a8","uuid":"0843d450-2c4a-4abf-b76b-16ffefd12534"}

The error shows:\n- Expected keys like: `action_in_proj.weight`\n- Got (unexpected) keys like: `model.action_in_proj.weight`\n\nThis is the LeRobot format vs OpenPI format issue mentioned in the task. I need to strip the `model.` prefix when loading the checkpoint.\n\nLooking at how OpenPI loads:\n```python\nsafetensors.torch.load_model(model, weight_path)\n\n\n```\n\nThis loads directly from a safetensors file. The pi0_libero checkpoint at `/root/autodl-tmp/checkpoints/pi0_libero/model.safetensors` has keys prefixed with `model.`, and I'm realizing the checkpoint also contains normalization-related keys from the LeRobot policy wrapper that aren't part of the raw PI0 model itself.\n\nSince OpenPI expects just the raw model weights without these prefixes and wrapper components, I'm thinking the cleanest solution is to create a custom loader that instantiates the PI0Pytorch model directly from the OpenPI config, manually loads the safetensors file while stripping the `model.` prefix, and wraps it in a policy interface—or alternatively, preprocess the checkpoint to remove the prefix and normalization buffers upfront.\n\n```\n\npython\ndef load_pi0_for_collection(checkpoint_path, config, strip_prefix=False):\n    \"\"\"Load PI0 checkpoint, optionally stripping model prefix\"\"\"\n    model = PI0Pytorch.from_config(config)\n    weights = load_file(checkpoint_path)\n    \n    if strip_prefix:\n        weights = {k.replace('model.', ''): v for k, v in weights.items()}\n    \n    model.load_state_dict(weights)\n    return model\n```\n\nActually, let me check what the OpenPI loading code expects exactly to see if there's a simpler integration Creates the policy with the loaded model + norm stats from the original checkpoint\n\nActually, let me take a different approach. Instead of fighting with OpenPI's policy loading, let me modify `libero_integration.py`'s `load_pi0_for_collection` to handle the LeRobot checkpoint format directly. Let me trace through OpenPI's policy loading to see what the minimal interface looks like, then just point the loader at my converted checkpoint and make sure the norm stats are accessible where the code expects them.\n\nLooking at the loading flow, `create_trained_policy` pulls the model, loads norm_stats from the assets directory, and wraps everything into a Policy object—but my pi0_libero checkpoint doesn't have that assets structure, so I need to check how the original pi0_base collection handled {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-6","id":"msg_01RXAhCxL9RrrQefKooWkHWb","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01XHJhHdRfRDiupaPT2T3itT","name":"mcp__agent-ml-research__ssh_execute","input":{"server":"westd-34142","command":"cat > /root/meta_brain_lora_routing/phase0/collect_activations_v2.py << 'SCRIPT'\n\"\"\"\nCollect per-step pi0_libero activations for PAR Router v2 retraining.\n\nHandles LeRobot-format checkpoint (model.* prefix) by stripping prefix before loading.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport argparse\nimport collections\nimport logging\nimport os\nimport random\nimport sys\nimport types\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nimport numpy as np\nimport torch\nimport safetensors.torch\n\nfrom configs import (\n    CLASS_6_TO_3_MAPPING,\n    PERTURBATION_TYPES_6CLASS,\n)\n\nlog = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n\n\ndef _ensure_openpi_imports():\n    \"\"\"Stub out lerobot before importing OpenPI.\"\"\"\n    for mod in [\n        \"lerobot\", \"lerobot.common\", \"lerobot.common.datasets\",\n        \"lerobot.common.datasets.lerobot_dataset\",\n    ]:\n        if mod not in sys.modules:\n            sys.modules[mod] = types.ModuleType(mod)\n\n\ndef load_pi0_libero(checkpoint_path: str, device: str):\n    \"\"\"Load pi0_libero with model.* prefix stripping.\"\"\"\n    _ensure_openpi_imports()\n\n    from openpi.training import config as _config\n    from openpi.policies import policy_config\n    from openpi.models import model as _model\n    import openpi.models.pi0 as pi0_pytorch\n\n    train_config = _config.get_config(\"pi0_libero\")\n\n    # Create model\n    model = pi0_pytorch.PI0Pytorch(config=train_config.model)\n\n    # Load weights with prefix stripping\n    weight_path = os.path.join(checkpoint_path, \"model.safetensors\")\n    tensors = safetensors.torch.load_file(weight_path)\n\n    skip_prefixes = ('normalize_inputs.', 'normalize_targets.', 'unnormalize_outputs.')\n    cleaned = {}\n    for k, v in tensors.items():\n        if any(k.startswith(p) for p in skip_prefixes):\n            continue\n        cleaned[k.removeprefix('model.')] = v\n\n    missing, unexpected = model.load_state_dict(cleaned, strict=False)\n    if missing:\n        log.warning(\"Missing keys: %d (first 5: %s)\", len(missing), missing[:5])\n    if unexpected:\n        log.warning(\"Unexpected keys: %d (first 5: %s)\", len(unexpected), unexpected[:5])\n\n    model.paligemma_with_expert.to_bfloat16_for_selected_params(\"bfloat16\")\n\n    # Create policy with transforms (norm_stats from assets or None)\n    data_config = train_config.data.create(train_config.assets_dirs, train_config.model)\n\n    # Try to load norm_stats from training assets\n    norm_stats = None\n    try:\n        from openpi.training import checkpoints as _checkpoints\n        norm_stats_path = train_config.assets_dirs / (data_config.asset_id or \"\")\n        if norm_stats_path.exists():\n            from openpi.shared import normalize as _normalize\n            norm_stats = _normalize.load(str(norm_stats_path))\n            log.info(\"Loaded norm stats from %s\", norm_stats_path)\n    except Exception as e:\n        log.warning(\"Could not load norm stats: %s\", e)\n\n    from openpi.policies import policy as _policy\n    from openpi.shared import transforms\n\n    policy = _policy.Policy(\n        model,\n        transforms=[\n            *data_config.data_transforms.inputs,\n            transforms.Normalize(norm_stats, use_quantiles=data_config.use_quantile_norm),\n            *data_config.model_transforms.inputs,\n        ],\n        output_transforms=[\n            *data_config.model_transforms.outputs,\n            transforms.Unnormalize(norm_stats, use_quantiles=data_config.use_quantile_norm),\n            *data_config.data_transforms.outputs,\n        ],\n        is_pytorch=True,\n        pytorch_device=device,\n    )\n\n    log.info(\"Loaded pi0_libero on %s\", device)\n    return policy, model\n\n\n# Import after path setup\nfrom libero_integration import (\n    create_libero_env,\n    obs_to_model_input,\n    LIBERO_DUMMY_ACTION,\n    MAX_STEPS,\n    DEFAULT_REPLAN_STEPS,\n    DEFAULT_NUM_STEPS_WAIT,\n)\nfrom collect_activations import ActivationCollector\n\n\ndef run_episode_per_step(\n    policy, collector,\n    perturbation_type, device,\n    task_suite=\"libero_spatial\",\n    seed=42,\n    replan_steps=DEFAULT_REPLAN_STEPS,\n    num_steps_wait=DEFAULT_NUM_STEPS_WAIT,\n):\n    \"\"\"Run one episode, returning per-step activations (not averaged).\"\"\"\n    env, task_description, init_states, task_idx = create_libero_env(\n        task_suite_name=task_suite,\n        perturbation_type=perturbation_type,\n        seed=seed,\n    )\n    max_steps = MAX_STEPS.get(task_suite, 400)\n    step = 0\n    success = False\n\n    try:\n        trial_idx = random.randint(0, len(init_states) - 1)\n        obs = env.set_init_state(init_states[trial_idx])\n\n        for _ in range(num_steps_wait):\n            obs, _, _, _ = env.step(LIBERO_DUMMY_ACTION)\n\n        action_plan = collections.deque()\n        step_activations = []\n        done = False\n\n        while not done and step < max_steps:\n            if len(action_plan) == 0:\n                collector.clear()\n                obs_dict = obs_to_model_input(obs, task_description)\n                result = policy.infer(obs_dict)\n                action_chunk = result[\"actions\"]\n                action_plan.extend(action_chunk[:replan_steps])\n\n                pooled = collector.get_pooled()\n                if pooled:\n                    step_activations.append(pooled)\n\n            action = action_plan.popleft()\n            if isinstance(action, torch.Tensor):\n                action = action.cpu().numpy()\n            obs, reward, done, info = env.step(\n                action.tolist() if hasattr(action, \"tolist\") else list(action)\n            )\n            step += 1\n            if done or reward > 0:\n                success = info.get(\"success\", reward > 0)\n    finally:\n        env.close()\n\n    return {\n        \"step_activations\": step_activations,\n        \"num_replan_points\": len(step_activations),\n        \"task_name\": f\"{task_suite}/task_{task_idx}\",\n        \"task_description\": task_description,\n        \"num_steps\": step,\n        \"success\": success,\n    }\n\n\ndef make_labels(perturbation_type):\n    if perturbation_type == \"nominal\":\n        return {\"label_6class\": -1, \"label_3class\": -1, \"label_binary\": 0}\n    label_6 = PERTURBATION_TYPES_6CLASS[perturbation_type]\n    label_3 = CLASS_6_TO_3_MAPPING[label_6]\n    return {\"label_6class\": label_6, \"label_3class\": label_3, \"label_binary\": 1}\n\n\ndef collect(args):\n    random.seed(args.seed)\n    np.random.seed(args.seed)\n    torch.manual_seed(args.seed)\n\n    output_dir = Path(args.output_dir)\n    output_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n\n    log.info(\"Loading pi0_libero from %s on %s\", args.checkpoint, args.device)\n    policy, model = load_pi0_libero(args.checkpoint, args.device)\n\n    collector = ActivationCollector(model)\n    collector.register_hooks()\n\n    if args.perturbation_type == \"all\":\n        perturbation_types = list(PERTURBATION_TYPES_6CLASS.keys())\n    else:\n        perturbation_types = [args.perturbation_type]\n\n    for ptype in perturbation_types:\n        is_nominal = ptype == \"nominal\"\n        log.info(\"Collecting %d episodes for %s\", args.num_episodes, ptype)\n        episodes = []\n\n        for ep_idx in range(args.num_episodes):\n            log.info(\"  Episode %d/%d\", ep_idx + 1, args.num_episodes)\n            try:\n                result = run_episode_per_step(\n                    policy, collector,\n                    perturbation_type=None if is_nominal else ptype,\n                    device=args.device,\n                    task_suite=args.task_suite,\n                    seed=args.seed + ep_idx,\n                )\n            except Exception:\n                log.exception(\"Episode %d failed, skipping\", ep_idx)\n                continue\n\n            labels = make_labels(ptype)\n            episode_data = {\n                \"step_activations\": result[\"step_activations\"],\n                \"num_replan_points\": result[\"num_replan_points\"],\n                **labels,\n                \"metadata\": {\n                    \"perturbation_type\": ptype,\n                    \"task_name\": result[\"task_name\"],\n                    \"num_steps\": result[\"num_steps\"],\n                    \"success\": result[\"success\"],\n                    \"episode_idx\": ep_idx,\n                },\n            }\n            episodes.append(episode_data)\n\n        save_path = output_dir / f\"{ptype}.pt\"\n        torch.save(episodes, save_path)\n        total_steps = sum(ep[\"num_replan_points\"] for ep in episodes)\n        log.info(\"Saved %s: %d episodes, %d total steps\", save_path, len(episodes), total_steps)\n\n    collector.remove_hooks()\n    log.info(\"Collection complete.\")\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    logging.basicConfig(\n        level=logging.INFO,\n        format=\"%(asctime)s [%(levelname)s] %(name)s: %(message)s\",\n    )\n    p = argparse.ArgumentParser()\n    p.add_argument(\"--checkpoint\", type=str,\n                   default=\"/root/autodl-tmp/checkpoints/pi0_libero\")\n    p.add_argument(\"--output_dir\", type=str,\n                   default=\"/root/meta_brain_lora_routing/activations_v2/per_step\")\n    valid_types = list(PERTURBATION_TYPES_6CLASS.keys()) + [\"all\", \"nominal\"]\n    p.add_argument(\"--perturbation_type\", type=str, default=\"all\", choices=valid_types)\n    p.add_argument(\"--num_episodes\", type=int, default=25)\n    p.add_argument(\"--task_suite\", type=str, default=\"libero_spatial\",\n                   choices=[\"libero_spatial\", \"libero_object\", \"libero_goal\", \"libero_10\", \"libero_90\"])\n    p.add_argument(\"--device\", type=str, default=\"cuda:1\")\n    p.add_argument(\"--seed\", type=int, default=42)\n    args = p.parse_args()\n    collect(args)\nSCRIPT","account":"root"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2128,"cache_read_input_tokens":73163,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":2128},"output_tokens":8,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"436bc48d-6197-44c5-bb0b-d9d9fb2582a8","uuid":"fc3a8e3b-1a6a-4000-bd56-b2b8597a5ea4"}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-6","id":"msg_01Ht2iG956ig42iFxPjY7v6z","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01EdhHMtigtQj7S813377E9V","name":"mcp__agent-ml-research__ssh_execute","input":{"server":"westd-34142","command":"cat > /root/meta_brain_lora_routing/phase0/collect_activations_v2.py << 'SCRIPT'\n\"\"\"\nCollect per-step pi0_libero activations for PAR Router v2 retraining.\nHandles LeRobot-format checkpoint (model.* prefix) by stripping prefix.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport argparse\nimport collections\nimport logging\nimport os\nimport random\nimport sys\nimport types\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nimport numpy as np\nimport torch\nimport safetensors.torch\n\nfrom configs import (\n    CLASS_6_TO_3_MAPPING,\n    PERTURBATION_TYPES_6CLASS,\n)\n\nlog = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n\n\ndef _ensure_openpi_imports():\n    for mod in [\n        \"lerobot\", \"lerobot.common\", \"lerobot.common.datasets\",\n        \"lerobot.common.datasets.lerobot_dataset\",\n    ]:\n        if mod not in sys.modules:\n            sys.modules[mod] = types.ModuleType(mod)\n\n\ndef load_pi0_libero(checkpoint_path: str, device: str):\n    \"\"\"Load pi0_libero with model.* prefix stripping, bypassing norm_stats.\"\"\"\n    _ensure_openpi_imports()\n\n    from openpi.training import config as _config\n    from openpi.models_pytorch import pi0_pytorch\n    import openpi.policies.policy as _policy\n    import openpi.transforms as transforms\n\n    train_config = _config.get_config(\"pi0_libero\")\n\n    # Create model and load weights with prefix stripping\n    model = pi0_pytorch.PI0Pytorch(config=train_config.model)\n    weight_path = os.path.join(checkpoint_path, \"model.safetensors\")\n    tensors = safetensors.torch.load_file(weight_path)\n\n    skip_prefixes = ('normalize_inputs.', 'normalize_targets.', 'unnormalize_outputs.')\n    cleaned = {}\n    for k, v in tensors.items():\n        if any(k.startswith(p) for p in skip_prefixes):\n            continue\n        cleaned[k.removeprefix('model.')] = v\n\n    missing, unexpected = model.load_state_dict(cleaned, strict=False)\n    if missing:\n        log.warning(\"Missing keys: %d (%s)\", len(missing), missing[:3])\n    if unexpected:\n        log.warning(\"Unexpected keys: %d\", len(unexpected))\n\n    model.paligemma_with_expert.to_bfloat16_for_selected_params(\"bfloat16\")\n\n    # Build policy with minimal transforms (no norm_stats for activation collection)\n    data_config = train_config.data.create(train_config.assets_dirs, train_config.model)\n\n    policy = _policy.Policy(\n        model,\n        transforms=[\n            *data_config.data_transforms.inputs,\n            transforms.Normalize(None, use_quantiles=False),\n            *data_config.model_transforms.inputs,\n        ],\n        output_transforms=[\n            *data_config.model_transforms.outputs,\n            transforms.Unnormalize(None, use_quantiles=False),\n            *data_config.data_transforms.outputs,\n        ],\n        is_pytorch=True,\n        pytorch_device=device,\n    )\n\n    log.info(\"Loaded pi0_libero on %s (missing=%d keys)\", device, len(missing))\n    return policy, model\n\n\nfrom libero_integration import (\n    create_libero_env,\n    obs_to_model_input,\n    LIBERO_DUMMY_ACTION,\n    MAX_STEPS,\n    DEFAULT_REPLAN_STEPS,\n    DEFAULT_NUM_STEPS_WAIT,\n)\nfrom collect_activations import ActivationCollector\n\n\ndef run_episode_per_step(\n    policy, collector, perturbation_type, device,\n    task_suite=\"libero_spatial\", seed=42,\n    replan_steps=DEFAULT_REPLAN_STEPS,\n    num_steps_wait=DEFAULT_NUM_STEPS_WAIT,\n):\n    env, task_description, init_states, task_idx = create_libero_env(\n        task_suite_name=task_suite,\n        perturbation_type=perturbation_type,\n        seed=seed,\n    )\n    max_steps = MAX_STEPS.get(task_suite, 400)\n    step = 0\n    success = False\n\n    try:\n        trial_idx = random.randint(0, len(init_states) - 1)\n        obs = env.set_init_state(init_states[trial_idx])\n\n        for _ in range(num_steps_wait):\n            obs, _, _, _ = env.step(LIBERO_DUMMY_ACTION)\n\n        action_plan = collections.deque()\n        step_activations = []\n        done = False\n\n        while not done and step < max_steps:\n            if len(action_plan) == 0:\n                collector.clear()\n                obs_dict = obs_to_model_input(obs, task_description)\n                result = policy.infer(obs_dict)\n                action_chunk = result[\"actions\"]\n                action_plan.extend(action_chunk[:replan_steps])\n\n                pooled = collector.get_pooled()\n                if pooled:\n                    step_activations.append(pooled)\n\n            action = action_plan.popleft()\n            if isinstance(action, torch.Tensor):\n                action = action.cpu().numpy()\n            obs, reward, done, info = env.step(\n                action.tolist() if hasattr(action, \"tolist\") else list(action)\n            )\n            step += 1\n            if done or reward > 0:\n                success = info.get(\"success\", reward > 0)\n    finally:\n        env.close()\n\n    return {\n        \"step_activations\": step_activations,\n        \"num_replan_points\": len(step_activations),\n        \"task_name\": f\"{task_suite}/task_{task_idx}\",\n        \"task_description\": task_description,\n        \"num_steps\": step,\n        \"success\": success,\n    }\n\n\ndef make_labels(perturbation_type):\n    if perturbation_type == \"nominal\":\n        return {\"label_6class\": -1, \"label_3class\": -1, \"label_binary\": 0}\n    label_6 = PERTURBATION_TYPES_6CLASS[perturbation_type]\n    label_3 = CLASS_6_TO_3_MAPPING[label_6]\n    return {\"label_6class\": label_6, \"label_3class\": label_3, \"label_binary\": 1}\n\n\ndef collect(args):\n    random.seed(args.seed)\n    np.random.seed(args.seed)\n    torch.manual_seed(args.seed)\n\n    output_dir = Path(args.output_dir)\n    output_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n\n    log.info(\"Loading pi0_libero from %s on %s\", args.checkpoint, args.device)\n    policy, model = load_pi0_libero(args.checkpoint, args.device)\n\n    collector = ActivationCollector(model)\n    collector.register_hooks()\n\n    if args.perturbation_type == \"all\":\n        perturbation_types = list(PERTURBATION_TYPES_6CLASS.keys())\n    else:\n        perturbation_types = [args.perturbation_type]\n\n    for ptype in perturbation_types:\n        is_nominal = ptype == \"nominal\"\n        log.info(\"Collecting %d episodes for %s\", args.num_episodes, ptype)\n        episodes = []\n\n        for ep_idx in range(args.num_episodes):\n            log.info(\"  Episode %d/%d\", ep_idx + 1, args.num_episodes)\n            try:\n                result = run_episode_per_step(\n                    policy, collector,\n                    perturbation_type=None if is_nominal else ptype,\n                    device=args.device,\n                    task_suite=args.task_suite,\n                    seed=args.seed + ep_idx,\n                )\n            except Exception:\n                log.exception(\"Episode %d failed, skipping\", ep_idx)\n                continue\n\n            labels = make_labels(ptype)\n            episode_data = {\n                \"step_activations\": result[\"step_activations\"],\n                \"num_replan_points\": result[\"num_replan_points\"],\n                **labels,\n                \"metadata\": {\n                    \"perturbation_type\": ptype,\n                    \"task_name\": result[\"task_name\"],\n                    \"num_steps\": result[\"num_steps\"],\n                    \"success\": result[\"success\"],\n                    \"episode_idx\": ep_idx,\n                },\n            }\n            episodes.append(episode_data)\n\n        save_path = output_dir / f\"{ptype}.pt\"\n        torch.save(episodes, save_path)\n        total_steps = sum(ep[\"num_replan_points\"] for ep in episodes)\n        log.info(\"Saved %s: %d episodes, %d total steps\", save_path, len(episodes), total_steps)\n\n    collector.remove_hooks()\n    log.info(\"Collection complete.\")\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    logging.basicConfig(\n        level=logging.INFO,\n        format=\"%(asctime)s [%(levelname)s] %(name)s: %(message)s\",\n    )\n    p = argparse.ArgumentParser()\n    p.add_argument(\"--checkpoint\", type=str,\n                   default=\"/root/autodl-tmp/checkpoints/pi0_libero\")\n    p.add_argument(\"--output_dir\", type=str,\n                   default=\"/root/meta_brain_lora_routing/activations_v2/per_step\")\n    valid_types = list(PERTURBATION_TYPES_6CLASS.keys()) + [\"all\", \"nominal\"]\n    p.add_argument(\"--perturbation_type\", type=str, default=\"all\", choices=valid_types)\n    p.add_argument(\"--num_episodes\", type=int, default=25)\n    p.add_argument(\"--task_suite\", type=str, default=\"libero_spatial\",\n                   choices=[\"libero_spatial\", \"libero_object\", \"libero_goal\", \"libero_10\", \"libero_90\"])\n    p.add_argument(\"--device\", type=str, default=\"cuda:1\")\n    p.add_argument(\"--seed\", type=int, default=42)\n    args = p.parse_args()\n > /root/meta_brain_lora_routing/phase0/collect_activations_v2.py << 'SCRIPT'\n\"\"\"\nCollect per-step pi0_libero activations for PAR Router v2 retraining.\nHandles LeRobot-format checkpoint (model.* prefix) by stripping prefix.\nDisables torch.compile for inference compatibility.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport argparse\nimport collections\nimport dataclasses\nimport logging\nimport os\nimport random\nimport sys\nimport types\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nimport numpy as np\nimport torch\nimport torch._dynamo\nimport safetensors.torch\n\nfrom configs import (\n    CLASS_6_TO_3_MAPPING,\n    PERTURBATION_TYPES_6CLASS,\n)\n\nlog = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n\n# Disable torch.compile entirely\ntorch._dynamo.config.suppress_errors = True\n\n\ndef _ensure_openpi_imports():\n    for mod in [\n        \"lerobot\", \"lerobot.common\", \"lerobot.common.datasets\",\n        \"lerobot.common.datasets.lerobot_dataset\",\n    ]:\n        if mod not in sys.modules:\n            sys.modules[mod] = types.ModuleType(mod)\n\n\ndef load_pi0_libero(checkpoint_path: str, device: str):\n    \"\"\"Load pi0_libero with model.* prefix stripping, no torch.compile.\"\"\"\n    _ensure_openpi_imports()\n\n    from openpi.training import config as _config\n    from openpi.models_pytorch import pi0_pytorch\n    import openpi.policies.policy as _policy\n    import openpi.transforms as transforms\n\n    train_config = _config.get_config(\"pi0_libero\")\n\n    # Disable torch.compile by patching the model config\n    model_config = dataclasses.replace(train_config.model, pytorch_compile_mode=None)\n\n    # Create model without compilation\n    model = pi0_pytorch.PI0Pytorch(config=model_config)\n\n    # Load weights with prefix stripping\n    weight_path = os.path.join(checkpoint_path, \"model.safetensors\")\n    tensors = safetensors.torch.load_file(weight_path)\n\n    skip_prefixes = ('normalize_inputs.', 'normalize_targets.', 'unnormalize_outputs.')\n    cleaned = {}\n    for k, v in tensors.items():\n        if any(k.startswith(p) for p in skip_prefixes):\n            continue\n        cleaned[k.removeprefix('model.')] = v\n\n    missing, unexpected = model.load_state_dict(cleaned, strict=False)\n    if missing:\n        log.warning(\"Missing keys: %d (%s)\", len(missing), missing[:3])\n    if unexpected:\n        log.warning(\"Unexpected keys: %d\", len(unexpected))\n\n    model.paligemma_with_expert.to_bfloat16_for_selected_params(\"bfloat16\")\n    model.to(device)\n    model.eval()\n\n    # Build policy with minimal transforms\n    data_config = train_config.data.create(train_config.assets_dirs, train_config.model)\n\n    policy = _policy.Policy(\n        model,\n        transforms=[\n            *data_config.data_transforms.inputs,\n            transforms.Normalize(None, use_quantiles=False),\n            *data_config.model_transforms.inputs,\n        ],\n        output_transforms=[\n            *data_config.model_transforms.outputs,\n            transforms.Unnormalize(None, use_quantiles=False),\n            *data_config.data_transforms.outputs,\n        ],\n        is_pytorch=True,\n        pytorch_device=device,\n    )\n\n    log.info(\"Loaded pi0_libero on %s (missing=%d keys, no compile)\", device, len(missing))\n    return policy, model\n\n\nfrom libero_integration import (\n    create_libero_env,\n    obs_to_model_input,\n    LIBERO_DUMMY_ACTION,\n    MAX_STEPS,\n    DEFAULT_REPLAN_STEPS,\n    DEFAULT_NUM_STEPS_WAIT,\n)\nfrom collect_activations import ActivationCollector\n\n\ndef run_episode_per_step(\n    policy, collector, perturbation_type, device,\n    task_suite=\"libero_spatial\", seed=42,\n    replan_steps=DEFAULT_REPLAN_STEPS,\n    num_steps_wait=DEFAULT_NUM_STEPS_WAIT,\n):\n    env, task_description, init_states, task_idx = create_libero_env(\n        task_suite_name=task_suite,\n        perturbation_type=perturbation_type,\n        seed=seed,\n    )\n    max_steps = MAX_STEPS.get(task_suite, 400)\n    step = 0\n    success = False\n\n    try:\n        trial_idx = random.randint(0, len(init_states) - 1)\n        obs = env.set_init_state(init_states[trial_idx])\n\n        for _ in range(num_steps_wait):\n            obs, _, _, _ = env.step(LIBERO_DUMMY_ACTION)\n\n        action_plan = collections.deque()\n        step_activations = []\n        done = False\n\n        while not done and step < max_steps:\n            if len(action_plan) == 0:\n                collector.clear()\n                obs_dict = obs_to_model_input(obs, task_description)\n                result = policy.infer(obs_dict)\n                action_chunk = result[\"actions\"]\n                action_plan.extend(action_chunk[:replan_steps])\n\n                pooled = collector.get_pooled()\n                if pooled:\n                    step_activations.append(pooled)\n\n            action = action_plan.popleft()\n            if isinstance(action, torch.Tensor):\n                action = action.cpu().numpy()\n            obs, reward, done, info = env.step(\n                action.tolist() if hasattr(action, \"tolist\") else list(action)\n            )\n            step += 1\n            if done or reward > 0:\n                success = info.get(\"success\", reward > 0)\n    finally:\n        env.close()\n\n    return {\n        \"step_activations\": step_activations,\n        \"num_replan_points\": len(step_activations),\n        \"task_name\": f\"{task_suite}/task_{task_idx}\",\n        \"task_description\": task_description,\n        \"num_steps\": step,\n        \"success\": success,\n    }\n\n\ndef make_labels(perturbation_type):\n    if perturbation_type == \"nominal\":\n        return {\"label_6class\": -1, \"label_3class\": -1, \"label_binary\": 0}\n    label_6 = PERTURBATION_TYPES_6CLASS[perturbation_type]\n    label_3 = CLASS_6_TO_3_MAPPING[label_6]\n    return {\"label_6class\": label_6, \"label_3class\": label_3, \"label_binary\": 1}\n\n\ndef collect(args):\n    random.seed(args.seed)\n    np.random.seed(args.seed)\n    torch.manual_seed(args.seed)\n\n    output_dir = Path(args.output_dir)\n    output_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n\n    log.info(\"Loading pi0_libero from %s on %s\", args.checkpoint, args.device)\n    policy, model = load_pi0_libero(args.checkpoint, args.device)\n\n    collector = ActivationCollector(model)\n    collector.register_hooks()\n\n    if args.perturbation_type == \"all\":\n        perturbation_types = list(PERTURBATION_TYPES_6CLASS.keys())\n    else:\n        perturbation_types = [args.perturbation_type]\n\n    for ptype in perturbation_types:\n        is_nominal = ptype == \"nominal\"\n        log.info(\"Collecting %d episodes for %s\", args.num_episodes, ptype)\n        episodes = []\n\n        for ep_idx in range(args.num_episodes):\n            log.info(\"  Episode %d/%d\", ep_idx + 1, args.num_episodes)\n            try:\n                result = run_episode_per_step(\n                    policy, collector,\n                    perturbation_type=None if is_nominal else ptype,\n                    device=args.device,\n                    task_suite=args.task_suite,\n                    seed=args.seed + ep_idx,\n                )\n            except Exception:\n                log.exception(\"Episode %d failed, skipping\", ep_idx)\n                continue\n\n            labels = make_labels(ptype)\n            episode_data = {\n                \"step_activations\": result[\"step_activations\"],\n                \"num_replan_points\": result[\"num_replan_points\"],\n                **labels,\n                \"metadata\": {\n                    \"perturbation_type\": ptype,\n                    \"task_name\": result[\"task_name\"],\n                    \"num_steps\": result[\"num_steps\"],\n                    \"success\": result[\"success\"],\n                    \"episode_idx\": ep_idx,\n                },\n            }\n            episodes.append(episode_data)\n\n        save_path = output_dir / f\"{ptype}.pt\"\n        torch.save(episodes, save_path)\n        total_steps = sum(ep[\"num_replan_points\"] for ep in episodes)\n        log.info(\"Saved %s: %d episodes, %d total steps\", save_path, len(episodes), total_steps)\n\n    collector.remove_hooks()\n    log.info(\"Collection complete.\")\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    logging.basicConfig(\n        level=logging.INFO,\n        format=\"%(asctime)s [%(levelname)s] %(name)s: %(message)s\",\n    )\n    p = argparse.ArgumentParser()\n    p.add_argument(\"--checkpoint\", type=str,\n                   default=\"/root/autodl-tmp/checkpoints/pi0_libero\")\n    p.add_argument(\"--output_dir\", type=str,\n                   default=\"/root/meta_brain_lora_routing/activations_v2/per_step\")\n    valid_types = list(PERTURBATION_TYPES_6CLASS.keys()) + [\"all\", \"nominal\"]\n    p.add_argument(\"--perturbation_type\", type=str, default=\"all\", choices=valid_types)\n    p.add_argument(\"--num_episodes\", type=int, default=25)\n    p.add_argument(\"--task_suite\", type=str, default=\"libero_spatial\",\n                   choices=[\"libero_spatial\", \"libero_object\", \"libero_goal\", \"libero_10\", \"libero_90\"])\n    p.add_argument(\"--device\", type=str, default=\"cuda:1\")\n    p.add_argument(\"--seed\", type=int, default=42)\n    args = p.parse_args()\n    collect(args)\nSCRIPT","account":"root"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":248,"cache_read_input_tokens":96819,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":248},"output_tokens":8,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"436bc48d-6197-44c5-bb0b-d9d9fb2582a8","uuid":"ec3e5f65-f88c-4ea5-90b4-3dd522d034e0"}

The `LiberoInputs` transform expects keys like `\"observation/image\"`, `\"observation/wrist_image\"`, `\"observation/state\"` (which come from the LeRobot repack transforms). But `obs_to_model_input` in `libero_integration.py` produces different keys.\n\nThe issue is that the original `load_pi0_for_collection` uses the full OpenPI policy pipeline which includes the repack transforms (from dataset format to inference format). But `obs_to_model_input` already formats things for the inference format directly (with keys like `\"image\"`, etc.).\n \nSo the flow is: `obs_to_model_input` creates the dict in inference format, then `policy.infer()` applies the input transforms (data_transforms → normalize → model_transforms), and the model's `sample_actions` processes the transformed input. I need to trace through what `data_transforms.inputs` actually expects to understand the key mismatch.\n\nThe problem is that `obs_to_model_input` produces inference-ready data, but my policy's transforms include `data_transforms.inputs` which expects dataset format keys like `\"observation/image\"`. The original `load_pi0_for_collection` must have handled this correctly, and looking at `create_trained_policy`, it applies `repack_transforms.inputs` first to convert from dataset format, then the data transforms — so I need to check if I'm missing that repacking step. produce what the full transform chain expects. The repack transforms are specifically for converting dataset format to inference format during training, so during inference the user provides data already in inference format and these transforms are either empty or identity operations. Looking back at `create_trained_policy`, the repack transforms default to an empty group if not provided, which confirms they're not needed during inference.\n\nSo the flow is straightforward: the input goes directly to LiberoInputs, which expects keys like `observation/image`, `observation/wrist_image`, and `observation/state`. The `obs_to_model_input` function takes the observation data and task description, then returns a dictionary with those exact keys plus the prompt, which LiberoInputs then transforms into the model's expected input format.\n\nNow I'm checking what the model_transforms actually do. If LiberoInputs is working correctly, the images should still be in HWC uint8 format when they reach model_transforms, and something like ResizeImages probably handles both the resizing and the HWC to {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-6","id":"msg_01X7uncWqaz4EXHmmKjUqSS2","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"thinking","thinking":"OK so now I understand the full transforms pipeline for PI0:\n\n1. **data_transforms.inputs**: `LiberoInputs(model_type=PI0)` - converts observation/image, observation/wrist_image, observation/state into `image: {base_0_rgb: HWC, left_wrist_0_rgb: HWC, ...}`, `state: ...`\n2. **model_transforms.inputs**: \n   - `InjectDefaultPrompt(None)`\n   - `ResizeImages(224, 224)` - resizes images with pad (still HWC)\n   - `TokenizePrompt(PaligemmaTokenizer)` - tokenizes the prompt text\n   - `PadStatesAndActions(action_dim=32)` - pads state and action arrays to consistent dimensions\n\nBut I'm not seeing the HWC→CHW conversion anywhere in the transforms pipeline. The model itself must be handling this conversion, or it's happening in the policy inference code. vision encoder's patch embedding layer.\n\nLet me trace through the `_preprocess_observation` or `embed_prefix` methods in PI0Pytorch to find where this permutation
